# MCAS Cherry Point Air Traffic Tower and Air Operations HVAC/BAS - ground truth

complete_with_documented_source_limitations

Source: [pdf/01__vol2__001__Cherry_Point_Air_Traffic_Tower_and_Air_Operations.pdf](../../pdf/01__vol2__001__Cherry_Point_Air_Traffic_Tower_and_Air_Operations.pdf). 75 supplied pages. SHA-256 `9b83f64b9ef98c22a4053ab4e79665fe7581a620c2908c2239b9216c02e2c0bf`.

Complete ground truth for the supplied 75-page Cherry Point HVAC/BAS source set: all pages visually reviewed; verified title-block/sheet/scale context; 53 literal schedule/valve/construction tables (440 rows); a nonduplicated 243-mark primary equipment registry; 167 literal control-valve schedule rows; 546 dual-engine-checked BAS point-template rows across 77 tables; BACnet network topology; written sequences and control diagrams; sensor/thermostat context; four piping schematics; and source limitations. The record does not fabricate installed plan quantities, final I/O/controller-card/wiring counts, addresses, or damper-actuator schedule specifications absent from the source.

## Reading this record

- Every supplied page has a retained full-page Poppler render and a completed visual finding. All strict schedule, point and source-text assertion cells are rechecked against retained MuPDF and Poppler captures during build/verify.
- The full schedule assembly preserves 53 source tables / 440 literal rows. The primary identity registry resolves 243 scheduled marks without treating schedule-only device-type tables as installed instance counts.
- The BAS source ledger preserves 546 typed template occurrences: 210 AI, 189 DI, 66 AO and 81 DO. Reused tag names remain distinct by printed source row and scope; this is not an installed terminal total.
- MI701 publishes BACnet IP, BACnet MS/TP, BACnet MS/TP UUKL and generic-network legend entries alongside three building DDC/cabinet contexts and named controllers. Network addressing and final panel/card/terminal design are not published.
- Cherry Point has 167 literal control-valve schedule rows with GPM/size/configuration/Cv fields. It has no standalone damper-actuator schedule; its damper construction/details and BAS behavior are retained without inventing actuator specifics.

## Project, scale, and supplied-sheet ledger

- project name as printed: FY20 P-228 ATC TOWER AND AIR OPERATIONS
- location as printed: MCAS CHERRY POINT, CHERRY POINT, NC
- navfac command as printed: NAVAL FACILITIES ENGINEERING COMMAND - MID-ATLANTIC
- firm as printed in title block: BURNS & MCDONNELL
- issue as printed: ISSUED FOR CONSTRUCTION
- issue date as printed: 11/19/19
- project number as printed: 1596300

- printed view scales observed:
  - 1/16 in = 1 ft-0 in
  - 3/32 in = 1 ft-0 in
  - 1/8 in = 1 ft-0 in
  - 1/4 in = 1 ft-0 in
  - 1/2 in = 1 ft-0 in
- takeoff scale status: Multiple printed view scales and graphic scale bars are visually present on plan/section/enlarged-plan sheets; no single document-wide scale is asserted.
- bar scale status: Graphic bars were visually observed on the inspected plan/section/enlarged-plan sheets (for example MS101/MS102, MH/MP plans, M-301/M-311/M-321, M-401/M-411/M-421). Details, schedules, controls and schematic sheets are not scale-derived unless a local scale is printed.

## Metadata

Complete full-page visual review for the supplied 75-page Cherry Point Air Traffic Tower and Air Operations mechanical and controls source. The record preserves source-bounded schedule identities/specifications, point-list templates, control/network/sequence information and hydronic schematics. It does not infer installed quantities from plan symbols, reuse a template row as a field-device count, or invent unavailable actuator/card/address data.

- module role: Final project, scale and complete 75-page visual-review context for the Cherry Point HVAC/BAS source set. It deliberately separates plan/schematic visual context from schedule and BAS template ledgers.

  - 1. PROVIDE UNIT WITH UNIT MOUNTED DISCONNECT AND 120V/60HZ/1PH CONTROL VOLTAGE TRANSFORMER.
  - 2. NUMBER OF MOTORS IN PARENTHESIS.
  - 3. PROVIDE UNITS WITH FILTER RACK ON THE UNIT INTAKE WITH A 2" MERV 8 FILTER.
  - 4. PROVIDE WITH EC TYPE MOTOR. EC MOTORS SHALL BE PROVIDED WITH EQUIPMENT TO PREVENT ADDING HARMONICS TO ELECTRICAL SYSTEM (INDUCTOR, ETC.) AND SHALL BE PROGRAMMED TO PREVENT EC MOTOR OVERSPEED.
  - 5. PROVIDE WITH CONDENSATE PUMP. PUMP CAPACITY SHALL BE 10 GPH AT 20 FT WC. CONDENSATE PUMP SHALL BE POWERED BY FCU ELECTRICAL CONNECTION.
  - 6. MOTOR CAN BE EITHER EC TYPE OR TWO SPEED. IF EC TYPE, PROVIDE MOTOR WITH EQUIPMENT TO PREVENT ADDING HARMONICS TO ELECTRICAL SYSTEM (INDUCTOR, ETC.) AND SHALL BE PROGRAMMED TO PREVENT EC MOTOR OVERSPEED.
  - 7. PROVIDE WITH EITHER MOTOR WITH VFD OR EC TYPE MOTOR. EC MOTORS SHALL BE PROVIDED WITH EQUIPMENT TO PREVENT ADDING HARMONICS TO ELECTRICAL SYSTEM (INDUCTOR, ETC.) AND SHALL BE PROGRAMMED TO PREVENT EC MOTOR OVERSPEED.
  - 8. PROVIDE UNITS WITH FILTER RACK ON THE UNIT INTAKE WITH BOTH A 2" MERV 8 PRE-FILTER, AND A MERV 11 FINAL FILTER.

- module role: Primary non-duplicated schedule mark registry for Cherry Point equipment identity presentation. All paired/secondary schedule tables remain in schedules_rank01_assembled.json and are not discarded.
- secondary mark tables:
  - Record:
    - table: AIR-OPS-DOAH-2

  - Record:
    - table: M612-DOAH-M1-PART-2
    - reason: same equipment mark is detailed in the paired primary schedule table or is repeated sound-power data
- registry count semantics: A source mark establishes only its printed schedule identity and classification. GRD and vibration/secondary contexts are not converted into installed plan, actuator, controller or field-I/O quantities.

  - sequence air ops mtracon doah:
    - document id: 01__vol2__001
    - status: partial_document_audit
    - module role: Full-sheet MI703 Air Ops/MTRACON dedicated-outdoor-air-handling-unit sequence context. This captures the written operating behavior and printed setpoints; it does not assign the reusable DOAH points list to undocumented field controller terminals.
    - source sheet: MI703
    - source page: 53
    - visual review:
      - pages:
        - 53
      - render: reviews/01__vol2__001/p53-p53-controls-poppler.png
      - dpi: 180
      - finding: MI703 was visually reviewed full page. It contains two columns of written DOAH operation, safeties, alarms and per-DOAH setpoints; no formal equipment-instance terminal-wiring schedule is present.
    - sequences:
      - Record:
        - id: ddc-and-fan-hoa
        - scope: Air Ops/MTRACON DOAH
        - finding: Install DDC hardware for the sequence/points; modulating control is PI unless specified otherwise. Supply/exhaust fans and VFDs receive fire-alarm precedence and have integral hand-off-auto switches; hand runs continuously subject to safeties, off stops, and auto uses start/stop plus DDC speed control.
        - assertion ids:
          - mi703-ddc-pi
          - mi703-fire-and-hoa
          - mi703-hoa-positions
      - Record:
        - id: occupancy-and-proofs
        - scope: Air Ops/MTRACON DOAH
        - finding: Scheduler input selects daytime occupied, 24/7 occupied or warm-up/cool-down. Proof/safety failure disables loops and commands the fan off until reset; printed proofs are supply/exhaust fan status and safeties include freezestat, high supply-duct pressure and supply-air smoke.
        - assertion ids:
          - mi703-occupancy
          - mi703-proofs
          - mi703-safeties
      - Record:
        - id: mode-airflow-control
        - scope: Air Ops/MTRACON DOAH
        - finding: Daytime mode enables fans/loops at scheduled maximum airflows; 24/7 mode does so at scheduled minimum airflows; warm-up/cool-down disables DOAH-A2. The VFD capacity loop targets the relevant max/min supply and exhaust airflow, with zero output and closed dampers when disabled.
        - assertion ids:
          - mi703-daytime
          - mi703-247
          - mi703-vfd-max
          - mi703-vfd-min
      - Record:
        - id: cooling-and-heating
        - scope: Air Ops/MTRACON DOAH
        - finding: Cooling is enabled above DOAH discharge setpoint, with enthalpy-controlled energy-recovery-wheel bypass and CHW valve control. Heating is enabled below that setpoint; wheel/bypass behavior precedes HHW-valve modulation, with frost detection and entering-air pump logic.
        - assertion ids:
          - mi703-cooling
          - mi703-cooling-wheel
          - mi703-heating
          - mi703-heating-wheel
      - Record:
        - id: freeze-and-pressure
        - scope: Air Ops/MTRACON DOAH
        - finding: When disabled below 40°F OA, the HHW valve maintains 40°F casing temperature and an inline circulator is energized; pressure relief uses differential airflow between outside-air and relief-air setpoints.
        - assertion ids:
          - mi703-freeze
          - mi703-relief
      - Record:
        - id: shutdown-loadshed-and-alarms
        - scope: Air Ops/MTRACON DOAH
        - finding: ESS/FACP shutdown disables the DOAH and closes applicable dampers. Chilled-water load shed disables listed units until the failed chiller is restored. MI703 prints process, maintenance and airflow/frost/humidity alarm conditions and per-unit setpoints.
        - assertion ids:
          - mi703-ess
          - mi703-fire-shutdown
          - mi703-load-shed
          - mi703-alarms
          - mi703-doah-a1-setpoints
          - mi703-doah-m1-setpoints
    - source limitations:
      - The sheet specifies points by reference to a points schedule but does not print a field controller terminal assignment or a per-point owner/wiring schedule.
      - MI703 applies to Air Ops/MTRACON DOAH behavior as printed; it does not itself establish an installed point multiplier or merge Air Ops and MTRACON equipment identities.
    - tables: []

  - sequences air ops and shared hydronics:
    - document id: 01__vol2__001
    - status: partial_document_audit
    - module role: Full-page sequence context for MI705, MI711, MI713, MI721 and MI723. It preserves high-value written AHU, chilled-water and heating-hot-water behavior without converting sequence prose into unprinted installed I/O, controller terminals or plan quantities.
    - source sheets:
      - MI705
      - MI711
      - MI713
      - MI721
      - MI723
    - source pages:
      - 55
      - 57
      - 59
      - 61
      - 63
    - visual review:
      - pages:
        - 55
        - 57
        - 59
        - 61
        - 63
      - renders:
        - reviews/01__vol2__001/p55-p55-controls-poppler.png
        - reviews/01__vol2__001/p57-p57-controls-poppler.png
        - reviews/01__vol2__001/p59-p59-controls-poppler.png
        - reviews/01__vol2__001/p61-p61-controls-poppler.png
        - reviews/01__vol2__001/p63-p63-controls-poppler.png
      - dpi: 180
      - finding: All five full-page sequence sheets were visually reviewed. They are written sequence sources with no formal controller-terminal schedule.
    - sequences:
      - Record:
        - id: air-ops-mtracon-ahu
        - sheet: MI705
        - finding: AHU H-O-A, occupancy, proofs/safeties, duct-static and supply-temperature reset, VAV relationships, return-air isolation, load shed, alarms and setpoints are specified for Air Ops/MTRACON.
        - assertion ids:
          - mi705-aho-auto
          - mi705-vav-static-reset
          - mi705-sat-reset
          - mi705-setpoints
      - Record:
        - id: air-ops-chw
        - sheet: MI711
        - finding: Air Ops variable-primary CHW system uses master chiller plant sequencing, 90–95% critical-valve pressure reset, bypass minimum-flow control, seven-day lead rotation, failure staging/load shed and 45°F CHW supply setpoint.
        - assertion ids:
          - mi711-bacnet-master
          - mi711-chiller-sequence
          - mi711-pump-dp
          - mi711-bypass
          - mi711-setpoints
      - Record:
        - id: air-ops-hhw
        - sheet: MI713
        - finding: Air Ops HHW plant enables from valve demand, stages boiler/pumps, resets differential pressure from critical valve position, uses bypass minimum flow, resets HHWS 130°F to 110°F against OA and rotates lead equipment every seven days.
        - assertion ids:
          - mi713-enable
          - mi713-boiler
          - mi713-dp
          - mi713-oa-reset
      - Record:
        - id: mtracon-atct-chw
        - sheet: MI721
        - finding: Shared MTRACON/ATCT CHW plant coordinates primary/secondary loops across both buildings, stages at primary/secondary-flow relationship, resets each building's differential-pressure setpoint from critical control-valve position, and prints 25-ft/50-ft MTRACON/ATCT initial pressure setpoints.
        - assertion ids:
          - mi721-shared-enable
          - mi721-stage
          - mi721-secondary-dp
          - mi721-setpoints
      - Record:
        - id: mtracon-atct-hhw
        - sheet: MI723
        - finding: Shared heat-recovery/boiler HHW plant operates across both buildings, requires heat-recovery control before boiler staging where available, uses 90–95% critical-valve differential-pressure control and printed MTRACON/ATCT pressure setpoints; it includes heat-recovery isolation above 140°F entering-water temperature.
        - assertion ids:
          - mi723-shared-enable
          - mi723-heat-recovery
          - mi723-secondary-dp
          - mi723-isolation
          - mi723-setpoints
    - source limitations:
      - These full-page sequence sheets establish written control behavior and some BAS integration context, but they do not provide a controller-panel terminal schedule, installed-point total or individual per-point owner/wiring mapping.
      - The shared MTRACON/ATCT sequences describe cross-building plant behavior; no source-derived alias is made between separate schedule/plan identities solely because a sequence references both buildings.
    - tables: []
    - assertions:
      - Record:
        - id: mi705-aho-auto
        - page: 55
        - bbox:
          - 170
          - 380
          - 780
          - 425
        - expected: WITH THE H-O-A SWITCH IN AUTO POSITION, THE SUPPLY FAN SHALL RUN SUBJECT TO THE SUPPLY FAN START/STOP SIGNAL (SF-SS) AND SAFETIES. FAN SPEED SHALL BE UNDER CONTROL OF THE DDC HARDWARE.
        - mode: contains
      - Record:
        - id: mi705-vav-static-reset
        - page: 55
        - bbox:
          - 875
          - 395
          - 1500
          - 535
        - expected: ALL SERIES FAN POWERED VAV BOXES SERVED BY THE AHU SHALL BE POLLED. IF ANY SERIES FAN POWERED BOX AIR PRIMARY AIR DAMPER IS AT 50% OPEN OR LESS, THE STATIC PRESSURE SETPOINT (SA-P-SP) SHALL BE RESET DOWNWARD SLOWLY
        - mode: contains
      - Record:
        - id: mi705-sat-reset
        - page: 55
        - bbox:
          - 875
          - 550
          - 1500
          - 650
        - expected: THE AHU SUPPLY AIR TEMPERATURE SETPOINT (SA-T-SP) SHALL BE RESET UPWARD SLOWLY
        - mode: contains
      - Record:
        - id: mi705-setpoints
        - page: 55
        - bbox:
          - 875
          - 958

    - id: ventilation-cooling-enable
    - page: 70
    - bbox:
      - 160
      - 500
      - 790
      - 550
    - mode: contains
    - expected: WHEN ZN-T RISES ABOVE ZN-T-SP-HL (WITH A 2 DEGREES F DEADBAND), ENABLE THE VENTILATION COOLING CONTROL LOOP
  - Record:
    - id: heating-enable
    - page: 70
    - bbox:
      - 160
      - 570
      - 790
      - 620
    - mode: contains
    - expected: WHEN ZN-T DROPS BELOW ZN-T-SP-LL (WITH A 3 DEGREES F DEADBAND), ENABLE THE HEATING CONTROL LOOP
  - Record:
    - id: source-safety-response
    - page: 70
    - bbox:
      - 160
      - 640
      - 790
      - 720
    - mode: contains
    - expected: FAILURE OF ANY PROOF OR ACTIVATION OF ANY SAFETY RESULT IN ALL CONTROL LOOPS BEING DISABLED AND THE FAN COIL UNIT FAN AND HUMIDIFIER BEING COMMANDED OFF UNTIL RESET
  - Record:
    - id: printed-proofs
    - page: 70
    - bbox:
      - 160
      - 738
      - 370
      - 805
    - mode: contains

  - Record:
    - id: reset-paths
    - page: 70
    - bbox:
      - 160
      - 822
      - 790
      - 873
    - mode: contains
    - expected: DDC HARDWARE RESET OF ALL PROOFS AND SAFETIES SHALL BE VIA A LOCAL BINARY PUSH-BUTTON (RST-BUT) INPUT TO THE DDC HARDWARE, VIA A REMOTE COMMAND TO THE DDC HARDWARE
  - Record:
    - id: ventilation-damper-sequence
    - page: 70
    - bbox:
      - 160
      - 892
      - 790
      - 944
    - mode: contains
    - expected: ONCE THE DAMPER POSITION IS PROVEN OPEN, ENERGIZE EXHAUST FAN. WHEN THIS LOOP IS DISABLED, THE EXHAUST FAN SHALL BE DE-ENERGIZED AND THE AFTER A 15 SECOND DELAY BOTH THE OA AND EA DAMPER SHALL CLOSE
  - Record:
    - id: heating-hhw-control
    - page: 70
    - bbox:
      - 160
      - 962
      - 790
      - 1016
    - mode: contains
    - expected: DDC HARDWARE SHALL ENERGIZE THE UNIT HEATER FANS AND MODULATE THE HHW VALVE TO MAINTAIN ZONE TEMPERATURE (ZN-T) AT SPACE LOW LIMIT SETPOINT (ZN-T-SP-LL) AS SHOWN
  - Record:
    - id: heating-disable-close
    - page: 70
    - bbox:
      - 160
      - 980
      - 790
      - 1016
    - mode: contains
    - expected: WHEN THIS LOOP IS DISABLED, THE UNIT HEATERS SHALL BE DE-ENERGIZED AND THE HHW VAVLES SHALL CLOSE
  - Record:

    - mode: contains

  - Record:

  - Record:

    - page: 71
    - bbox:

    - page: 71
    - bbox:

  - Record:
    - id: RANK01-ALL-SCHEDULE-TABLES
    - classification: 53 literal source schedule, valve and construction tables
    - source scope: schedules_rank01_assembled.tables
    - status: 440 source rows retained; table rows do not assert plan-placement quantity.
  - Record:
    - id: RANK01-SECONDARY-DOAH-SOUND-TABLES
    - classification: paired DOAH performance and fan sound-power source tables
    - source scope: schedule_identities_rank01.secondary_mark_tables
    - status: full cells retained but repeated marks not duplicated in equipment identity index.
  - Record:
    - id: RANK01-GRD-TYPE-SCHEDULES
    - classification: Air Operations and MTRACON air-device schedule type marks
    - source scope: AIR-OPS-GRILLE-REGISTER-DIFFUSER and M613-GRD-MARKS
    - status: type schedule rows, not installed outlet/damper-actuator quantities.
  - Record:
    - id: RANK01-CONTROL-VALVE-TABLES
    - classification: Air Operations, MTRACON and ATCT heating/chilled-water control-valve schedule rows
    - source scope: schedules_rank01_assembled valve tables
    - status: literal valve unit/mark, GPM, size, configuration and Cv fields retained.
- reconciliation findings:
  - Air Operations DOAH-A1/A2 and MTRACON DOAH-M1 are each printed across two source schedule parts; the first part establishes schedule identity and the companion part remains retained as performance/electrical detail.
  - Air Operations fan sound-power rows reuse AHU/DOAH marks already present in equipment schedules, so they remain supplemental acoustic detail rather than duplicate equipment identities.
  - The Air Operations and MTRACON GRD marks are type schedules. No plan-symbol count, actuator count or BAS terminal count is derived from them.
  - The supplied control-valve tables provide 167 literal valve rows across three building scopes. Their source table row is retained rather than treating a valve mark as a standalone physical device without its unit/scope context.

    - rows: 167
    - fields:

## Supporting Evidence

Full-set qualification and explicit source limitations for the Cherry Point HVAC/BAS ground-truth record.

- module role: Full-set qualification and explicit source limitations for the Cherry Point HVAC/BAS ground-truth record.
- source limitations:
  - The 243 primary identities are non-duplicated literal schedule marks, not an installed plan-symbol count. The assembled source schedule evidence preserves 53 source tables and 440 literal rows without applying a plan multiplier.
  - The 546 typed point-list rows are reusable source templates across 77 source tables: 210 AI, 189 DI, 66 AO and 81 DO occurrences. They are not final controller-card, terminal, wiring or installed-field-point quantities.
  - The source publishes BACnet IP, BACnet MS/TP and BACnet MS/TP UUKL network legends and named controller/cabinet contexts, but not controller make/model beyond source designations, addresses, subnet/VLAN design, final panel/card count or a field-wiring schedule.
  - The source has 167 literal control-valve schedule rows, with unit/valve mark, GPM, size, configuration and Cv. It has damper details and point/sequence behavior but no standalone damper-actuator schedule with manufacturer/model, torque, signal, fail action or installed quantity; those fields remain unavailable.
  - Literal point-template occurrences include seven SPACE TEMPERATURE rows and one SPACE THERMOSTAT row. The point-name ledger has no literal CO2 text row, although MI700 includes a CO2 monitor legend symbol. None of these template observations establishes a facility-wide installed sensor/thermostat count.
  - All 75 pages were reviewed visually at full page. MuPDF and Poppler independently recheck every authored literal schedule/point cell and bounded source assertion, but parser agreement does not prove physical plan quantities or fill unprinted BAS configuration fields.

    - engine: poppler

## Verification

The bundle verifier independently reruns MuPDF and Poppler checks for every authored literal table/assertion module and hashes all linked modules, the source PDF, manifest and cited full-page renders. It validates source evidence and annotation integrity; it does not automatically infer physical quantities or prove engineering suitability.

Re-run: `python3 ground_truth/tools/bundle_record.py verify ground_truth/records/01__vol2__001.json` from the collection directory.
